MonimerSerif_PERSONAL_USE_ONLY.otf
Note of the author
NOTE: This font is for PERSONAL USE ONLY!

To purchase a commercial license, visit:
https://www.mansgreback.com/product/monimer-serif

Monimer Serif is a characterful display serif with calligraphic flair — lively, slightly slanted forms, generous curves and crisp bracketed serifs. It reads classic and bookish yet warm, suited to editorial mastheads, book covers, packaging and refined branding.

For questions, please visit https://mansgreback.com/faq

First seen on DaFont: August 31, 2026
